## Source and computation notes

### A_ORIGINAL_SOURCE

# Original-source access and convention audit

## Anderson 1989: complete original obtained

The official Göttingen archive supplies the complete article at

https://gdz.sub.uni-goettingen.de/download/pdf/PPN356556735_0095/LOG_0011.pdf

The volume manifest is

https://manifests.sub.uni-goettingen.de/iiif/presentation/PPN356556735_0095/manifest

The downloaded PDF has 5,365,119 bytes and SHA-256
`bf656f919b6fb70b372b08a19beff97bcd1c0c89df715df09fa8fd1881f9bc67`.
Its first page is the archive cover. PDF page2 is printed page63, and PDF page70 is printed page131. The source was rendered and OCRed for inspection; only the article's relevant statements and proofs are reported as read. The complete PDF, rendered scans and complete OCR remain research inputs excluded from the deliverable archive.

The exact original anchors are:

| Result used | Original location | Inspection |
|---|---|---|
| Completed coefficient rings, cyclotomic action | §§1.3–1.4, pp.67–68 | Text |
| Unique adelic beta cocycle | Theorem1 and supplement, p.69 | Original scan |
| All-degree Fermat torsion representation | §§5.3–5.5, Proposition5.5.1, pp.86–89 | Text and original p.88 scan |
| Gamma coefficient ring and branches | §§7.1–7.4, pp.95–97; §3.1, p.73 | Text and original p.73 scan |
| Reciprocity and multiplication | Theorem2, p.98; proof pp.101–102; Corollary8.6.3, p.103 | Original theorem and corollary scans; proof text |
| Logarithmic derivative | Theorem5 and proof, pp.116–117 | Original scans and proof text |
| Kummer moments and power series | §11.6, p.117; Proposition11.6.1, p.118 | Original scans |
| Gauss sums | Theorem6, p.118; proof §§12.2–12.8, pp.119–124 | Original theorem scan; full proof text |
| Fundamental-group comparison | §13.1, p.125; Proposition13.3.2, p.126; §§13.4–13.6, pp.127–129 | Original statement and map scans; text |
| Odd moment expansion | §13.7, equation(13.7.3), p.130 | Original scan |

The source makes the coefficient/base-group scope explicit: its beta cocycle is defined on all of G_Q with values in Zhat[[Zhat(1)^2]]^×, and its gamma branches have coefficients in the product of the Witt-vector rings of the algebraic closures of the residue fields. Proposition5.5.1 supplies the semilinear action on torsion of every Fermat Jacobian. Theorem6 then gives the stated Gauss-sum interpolation. Proposition13.3.2 supplies the precise odd-prime fundamental-group comparison, retaining its basis coboundary; RemarkII supplies the cyclotomic-kernel equality also at two.

## Multiplication convention, derived explicitly

Use the compatible positive real roots of a positive integer n and the roots of unity exp(2πi/N), as in the manuscript. Define kappa_n(g) by the quotient of the Galois translate of n^(1/N) and n^(1/N).

In Corollary8.6.3 put a=ns. Its product indexed by nb=a is then the product indexed by b=s+c, nc=0. Division by Gamma_g(ns) and the product of Gamma_g(c) gives

\[
\frac{\prod_{nc=0}\Gamma_g(s+c)/\Gamma_g(c)}{\Gamma_g(ns)}
 =\exp(-2\pi i\,ns\,\kappa_n(g))\otimes1.
\]

Indeed, the exponent in Anderson's real power is the least nonnegative representative of −ns. It differs from −ns by an integer; the corresponding integer power of n is rational and fixed by g. The Galois quotient therefore has exactly the displayed negative exponent. The ratio is independent of the chosen representative of s, since changes are integral multiples of n kappa_n(g).

Anderson evaluates in Qbar⊗W. Ihara II, p.9, evaluates in W⊗Qab. The explicit ring isomorphism z⊗w ↦ w⊗z carries the preceding factor to 1⊗exp(−2πi ns kappa_n(g)). Ihara's character is fixed by (n^(−r))^(g−1)=exp(2πi r Psi_n^(0)(g)), hence Psi_n^(0)=−kappa_n. These two maps account for the tensor placement and sign in D.20.

## Moment convention, derived explicitly

Fix an odd prime ell and g in G_{Q(mu_{ell^infinity})}. Let c(g,zeta) be the Kummer exponent appearing in Anderson §11.6. Its underlying algebraic number is 1−zeta^(−1). Since

\[
1-\zeta^{-1}=\zeta^{-1}(\zeta-1),
\]

and every ell-power root of zeta is fixed by g, this Kummer character equals the character of zeta−1. Changing a root branch multiplies it by a root of unity fixed by this same group. This is the exact branch comparison used here.

For m>1 set

\[
S_n=\sum_{0<i<\ell^n}i^{m-1}c(g,\zeta_{\ell^n}^{i}),\qquad
U_n=\sum_{\substack{0<i<\ell^n\\\ell\nmid i}}
                  i^{m-1}c(g,\zeta_{\ell^n}^{i}).
\]

The terms with i=ell j give the exact identity

\[
S_n=U_n+\ell^{m-1}S_{n-1}.
\]

The limit of S_n is Anderson's beta_m. Modulo ell^n, U_n is the Kummer character of the product defining the manuscript's epsilon_n^(m); its limit is kappa_m. Therefore

\[
\kappa_m=(1-\ell^{m-1})\beta_m.
\]

Substitution into Anderson (13.7.3) gives the coefficient kappa_m/((1−ell^(m−1))m!) in D.8. The argument identifies characters rather than comparing a few polynomial coefficients.

For a general g, the comparison of series still retains the two cyclotomic factors S_g(u)S_g(v) and the basis ratio

\[
Q((1+u)^{\chi(g)}-1,(1+v)^{\chi(g)}-1)/Q(u,v)
\]

from Proposition13.3.2. On the cyclotomic kernel these factors are one. The inversion of the map on printed p.125 is explicitly corrected in Ihara II's Supplement, p.12; the same supplement states the inverse relation of the deck-action convention on Anderson p.85 to Ihara1986. Neither correction is omitted from the source comparison.

## Ihara 1986: original full text not obtained

The official article record, https://annals.math.princeton.edu/1986/123-1/p03 , confirms the article and pp.43–106 but supplied no full-text link. The author's official bibliography, https://www.kurims.kyoto-u.ac.jp/en/emeritus/ihara/papers.html , lists it as item25 without a file link. Crossref routes the DOI to JSTOR. OpenAlex and Semantic Scholar both returned no open full-text location.

The AMS Digital Mathematics Registry was opened at https://mathscinet.ams.org/dmr/JournalListA.html . Its Annals entries direct the historical run to JSTOR and list the Princeton and EMIS runs starting in2003 and1998, respectively. The Bielefeld digitization registry agrees on the historical repository; no official GDZ volume123 source was located. These catalogue results are evidence about the routes checked, not a proof that no open copy exists anywhere.

The original IharaII correction was obtained and read independently. It verifies its own correction statements, not an inspection of the unavailable1986 pages.

## Coleman 1989: original full text not obtained

The Mathematical Society of Japan's volume list and Crossref identify the official chapter at DOI10.2969/aspm/01710055. OpenAlex and Semantic Scholar both point to the Project Euclid chapter PDF. That provider's access challenge was not bypassed.

The current Berkeley author page failed, and the archived author preprint list was opened. The public Wayback CDX inventory of the author's site yielded no corresponding named research file. The Kyoto repository match was inspected and resolved to the unrelated one-page1986 item *Ramified Torsion Points on Curves*; it is not recorded as the requested paper.

Internet Archive's public catalogue identifies the full ASPM17 volume as `algebraicnumbert0000unse_g7s7`. Its public metadata explicitly marks the item access-restricted and the PDF/OCR private. Those files were not requested. This path therefore did not supply the original chapter either.

The access outcomes and machine-readable source URLs are retained in `a_original_source_access.json`. Anderson is now an original-source check; Ihara1986 and Coleman1989 remain explicitly unread originals.


## Herfort–Ribes1985 original: complete proof verified

The official GDZ volume manifest supplied article `LOG_0014` in volume358. The complete original PDF (733209bytes; SHA256 `67f1f99f001a6b198a3e5cef50849fbedd244e0469aa7172986d8c777ce126d8`) was downloaded and every original page155–161 was viewed. TheoremA′ (p157; complete proof pp157–159) gives torsion conjugacy for the required free profinite product. TheoremB′ (p159) gives factor malnormality and the exact centralizer equality. Theorem1 (p160) upgrades to finite subgroups of a two-factor product; Theorem2(i) on the same page is a valid more general compact-indexed version. Thus “Theorem2(i)” is not an erroneous citation, but A′ is the direct statement used for individual elliptic generators. The proof uses the open-subgroup Kurosh theorem, the torsion-free Cartesian kernel, and a finite wreath-product quotient; it does not assume a discrete reduced-word normal form for profinite elements.

Citation key: `A_HerfortRibes1985`. Source: https://gdz.sub.uni-goettingen.de/download/pdf/PPN243919689_0358/LOG_0014.pdf . The application to D.27 establishes necessity of elliptic conjugators for actual profinite automorphisms. The finite obstruction still concerns the selected homology action; changing that action requires a fresh computation.

## Main findings

The free subgroup Π₀ has index 12 in SL₂(ℤ), but its congruence level is 4. The additional lattice

`M₀ = {(c₀,c₁) ∈ ℤ² : c₀+c₁ = 0 modulo 3}`

has inverse image Π₀₀ of index 36 in SL₂(ℤ). It is the normal closure of δ = λ₁λ₀⁻¹, and also Π₀ intersected with the commutator subgroup of SL₂(ℤ). Its exact congruence level is 12. Chinese remainders identify its reduction with `V₄ × Q₈`, of order 32. The quaternion identification is certified by the unique involution and six order-four elements.

There are three different finite quotient maps, all given explicitly. SL₂(ℤ/12) has order 1152 and quotient of order 36 by this kernel. The actual reduction of GL₂(ℤ) has determinant ±1, order 2304, and quotient of order 72. The full GL₂(ℤ/12) has order 4608, and quotient of order 144. The commutator `[λ₀,λ₁] = [[1,4],[4,5]] modulo 12` proves that full reduction does not factor through the minimum metabelian modular quotient; reduction followed by quotient by the order-32 group does.

The corrected multiplier scheme is four open affine lines, and the corrected N is the explicit scalar semidirect product `Ga² ⋊ O₁₂*`. The lattice equation must be imposed in the vector group of M₀. Imposing it only after the map to M introduces a coordinate `z` with `3z=0`; the corrected N is its schematic closure `z=0` away from 3. The old fine scheme of scan 41 maps isomorphically to this corrected N over ℤ by `k=ν′−a₁`, `ν″=a₁`. This resolves the precise finite-stage comparison left in LM1 E.20, including bad-characteristic fibres.

The final normalizer bisection has explicit central cocycle `(a+b)b′ modulo 2`, the pullback of the dihedral extension `1 → C₂ → D₈ → C₂² → 1`. The early literal definition (171) reaches only the equal-sign two-component subgroup; its asserted surjection to all four components is false. The later formula (212) supplies the corrected full bisection. Actual central quotient morphisms relate the earlier and final versions of Γ and M.

Each of the three final distinguished subgroups is a graph isomorphic to Γ, with eight geometric components and relative dimension two. All are smooth and flat at both 2 and 3. Their point groups have orders 32 over F₂ and 72 over F₃, and the exact group laws and localization parametrizations are written in A.15. A.16 constructs character-base pullbacks from the §48 matrix groups and homomorphisms with specified norm-one kernels. The old and new fibres are then compared using these actual maps and coordinate rings.

For the standard Belyi/braid normalization, all of G_Q, GT-hat, and GT-hat₀ have level-12 matrix image

`{ [[μ,t],[0,1]] : μ = 1,5,7,11; t = 0,4,8 }`,

of order 12. Its genuine conjugation image in Aut(GL₂(ℤ/12)) also has order 12; its image in Out(GL₂(ℤ/12)) is trivial. Its canonical outer image in Out(SL₂(ℤ/12)) has order four, and its scalar image on M/12M has order four. The arithmetic kernel field is exactly `Q(ζ₁₂, cube_root(2))`. This equality is proved by the tangential Puiseux expansions of the Legendre three-division polynomial, independently of a pointwise Kummer-coordinate identification.

## Corrections to the source and prior notes

The pushout ring requires the factor N in the term Nxy. Equality of evaluations does not imply equality of sections. The full evaluation kernel retains all residue components, not only the N-torsion additive component. The integers ±5 are affine representatives rather than global integral unit sections. For the representative `(−5)^a 5^b`, the correct mixed coefficients in ν′ and ν″ are −6ab and −4ab. The second-pass note checking consistency of two later equations missed their incompatibility with this earlier product representative. The scan 61 signs, the scan 76 exponent, the literal scan 82 image, and the missing quotient in scan 87 have explicit mathematical corrections.

The final line of (194) without the question mark also requires correction: `N_rho / μ_rho` retains a C₃ kernel, whereas `N_rho^? / μ_rho` is γ. The invariant σ-line is generated by λ₀+λ₁, resolving the λ₁+λ₂ subscript in (204). In the expression `U=x u_μ`, the variable x belongs to the geometric group; inserting u_μ into x again is a type error and is replaced by the graph equation U=a.

## Independent audit of D.15–D.18

The projective CM field argument was checked separately. The ±1 quotient field contains the Weber values, so CM reciprocity places the full maximal abelian extension inside it; the commutative CM-linear Tate image gives the reverse inclusion. The residue-unit adjustment by μ₆/±1 and μ₄/±1 fills the stated individual target factors. The intersection argument is complete: two distinct abelian index-two subgroups force the derived group to have order at most two, and the splitting field of X⁴−3 realizes that nontrivial possibility over both imaginary quadratic fields. The Hilbert-90 local valuation-parity calculation and the saved finite norm tables agree.

The initial pointwise −8κ₂ identification had a possible shear ambiguity. This was sent to the D author and corrected by fixing positive real Tate roots and the Legendre branch-cut period ratio. The resulting choice matches the braid normalization in A.17; the local monodromy matrix alone would not have fixed it. With that correction, no further gap was found in these four claims.

## Exact comparison maps

We retain the source's arithmetic equation \(x_0^n+x_1^n+x_\infty^n=0\). The geometric comparison with \(x^n+y^n=z^n\) multiplies the third coordinate by a chosen \(\alpha_n\) with \(\alpha_n^n=-1\). For odd \(n\), \(\alpha_n=-1\) is rational. For \(n=4\), the displayed comparison is over \(\mathbb Q(\zeta_8)\); the plus quartic has no real points. These fields are retained in the arithmetic statements.

The cover \(q_n\) corresponds to \(K_n=\ker(F_2\to(\mathbb Z/n)^2)\). Its integral homology is the kernel of the Fox differential \((X-1,Y-1):R_n^2\to R_n\), and the exact covering comparison is
\[
0\longrightarrow R_n/\mathbb Z\mathcal N_n
\longrightarrow H_1(U_n,\mathbb Z)
\longrightarrow n\mathbb Z^2\longrightarrow0.
\]
Thus the finite affine homology includes the rank-two quotient. Its inverse limit is zero under the inclusion transition maps \(dn\widehat{\mathbb Z}^2\to n\widehat{\mathbb Z}^2\).

The compact homology has the integral presentation \(R_n/(S_X,S_Y,P_n)\), with \(P_n=\sum_{0\le j\le i<n}X^iY^j\). The quotient is obtained by the displayed peripheral-chain map; it is not inferred from complex characters. The transition formula
\[
P_{dn}\longmapsto dP_n+\binom d2\mathcal N_n
\]
makes the defining ideals pro-zero modulo every coefficient modulus. Together with a compactness proof comparing the closed commutator with the kernel homology tower, this proves the full \(\widehat{\mathbb Z}[[\widehat{\mathbb Z}^2]]\) rank-one assertion. No power-series-domain assumption or restriction to a pro-\(\ell\) component is used.

The generalized Jacobian \(\mathcal J_n\) is linked to the rank-two torus \(\mathcal J_1=T\) by the actual norm map. Its Tate realization has the exact sequence
\[
0\to(R_n/\mathbb Z\mathcal N_n)\otimes\widehat{\mathbb Z}
\to T_\bullet(\mathcal J_n)\to n\widehat M(1)\to0.
\]
At \(n=2\), the source has rank five, the target rank two and the kernel rank three. Modulo two the proposed full-realization identification would equate groups of orders 32 and 4. The finite example is recorded by program.

The CM ring map is \(R_n\to\operatorname{End}(J_n)\), with the same compact annihilator. Its rational image is the product of cyclotomic fields indexed by the permitted character orbits. This replaces the ambiguous expression \(\mathbb Z[\mu_n]^2\) by a specified homomorphism, kernel and image.

## Splitting calculation

The quotient by the central involution has the affine realization
\[
\rho=(R,0),\quad \sigma=(S,(1/3,-1/3)),\quad
R=\begin{pmatrix}0&-1\\1&-1\end{pmatrix},\
S=\begin{pmatrix}0&1\\1&0\end{pmatrix}.
\]
The corrected generators \(\lambda_0\rho,\sigma\) fix \(p=(2/3,1/3)\). The resulting cochain \(b_h=p-A_hp-t_h\) is integral and satisfies \(c+\delta b=0\). For the full unit action, with \(\nu=(\mu-1)/2\), the correcting translation is \((-\nu,-\nu)\). The corrected scalar lift is the homothety of ratio \(\mu\) about \(p\), so it commutes with the finite complement.

The cohomology lemma is proved integrally for every coefficient abelian group: \(H^0(S_3,I^3/I)=H^1(S_3,I^3/I)=0\). It therefore proves the unique conjugator for the full profinite product. The finite normalized cochain computation independently gives \(|H^2(S_3,M/N)|=\gcd(N,3)\); the actual extension class is zero even where that obstruction group is nonzero.

## Cuspidal extension and exact exponent

The extension class is the homomorphism from the degree-zero cusp lattice to the dual Jacobian taking a divisor to its line bundle. We prove this by pushing the torus extension out through each character and identifying the resulting Poincaré line bundle.

For every pair of distinct coordinate cusps \(P,Q\), a tangent-line ratio has divisor \(n(P-Q)\). At \(Q\), the pole semigroup is exactly \(\langle n-1,n\rangle\): those two pole orders are realized explicitly, and their semigroup has precisely the genus number of gaps. Every proper divisor of \(n\) is a gap. Therefore \([P-Q]\) has exact order \(n\), for every \(n\ge3\), including even levels.

The extension consequently has exact order \(n\). Pullback along \([n]\) is split, and projection from that split pullback gives an isogeny \(T_{D_n}\times J_n\to\mathcal J_n\) with kernel \(J_n[n]\) and degree \(n^{2g_n}\). The splitting is unique and descends to \(\mathbb Q\). There is no integer killing all levels.

Rohrlich's original Theorem 1, printed p.101, supplies the whole geometric cuspidal group:
\[
(\mathbb Z/n)^{3n-7}\quad(n\text{ odd}),\qquad
(\mathbb Z/n)^{3n-7}\times\mathbb Z/2\quad(n\text{ even}).
\]
Its definitions on p.95 identify the quotient with the image of the extension-class homomorphism. This is the requested reduction, rather than an attribution based on a secondary citation. At \(n=3,4,5,7\), the exact exponents are \(3,4,5,7\).

### D_ARITHMETIC_BOUNDARY_NORM

# D.55 — arithmetic boundary norm and compact orientation

## Actual arithmetic comparison

The compactification map is constructed on the actual G3 cover. Its cusp radical has dimension `n/36−1`, and its compact homology has dimension `2+5n/36`. The source action fixes every cusp homology class because the base action is identity and the cusp defect is central. Thus every allowed homology parameter induces a compact homology map.

For the literal cusp error `z`, the thirty-sixth power of `zt` contributes the cyclic norm of z. Summing over the actual cusp cosets contributes every element of G once. Applying the lifted source map to the marked punctured-surface relation therefore proves the identity

`norm(q(p)) = (wedge² T_c)(Theta) − Theta`.

The norm includes the actual compactification projection. Theta is the inverse-form orientation bivector, not the matrix of the bilinear form. No invertibility assumption is used in this identity.

\]

The canonical odd-\(A\) averaging idempotent splits \(Z=Z_0\oplus Z_-\), with
\[
 \dim Z_0=m^2(q-1)/2,\qquad \dim Z_-=w\rho/2.
\]
Both dimensions are positive. The new finite target is the actual class-two quotient modulo only \(Z_0\). Its exact common quotient with D.50 is \(\mathcal T_2/Z\). The central cusp correction is onto on \(Z_-\), giving the new joint source
\[
 \mathcal Y_h=\mathcal Z_h\times_{\mathcal T_2/Z}(\mathcal T_2/Z_0).
\]
Its projection to the complete D.50 source has kernel \(Z_-\). The action, both full conjugators, normalized parameter, CM pair, full metabelian action and global-sign congruence action are retained through this literal projection.

For the image \(H\) of the fixed-parameter kernel in the full class-two quotient, choose its odd complement \(\widetilde A\). The subgroup \(E=[D,\widetilde A]\) has the moving abelian image. Normality therefore places \(W\wedge V\) in \(E\cap D'\).

Their central ambiguity is an affine vector space over \(\mathbb F_2\). Averaging over the odd complement gives an actual invariant surjection. On commutators it is the exterior projection, with kernel exactly \(W\wedge V\).

The proof then establishes \([E,\widetilde A]=E\) inside this group: the moving abelian image supplies \(\bigwedge^2W\), and the remaining \(W\wedge V^A\) summand has no odd invariants. This proves that \(E\) is the exact pronilpotent residual of \(H\). An actual fourth-power adjustment in the original source removes any residual abelian coordinate divisible by four. It gives the exact image of the intersection with the complete homology kernel.

## Descent, generation and compatibility

The D.50 action descends to the common class-two quotient by a characteristic quotient of the image of \(K_3\). Its specified conjugators descend as actual elements.

Lifting those two elements and correcting in \(Z_-\) solves the exact cusp equation. ## Exact field comparison

Let r=(3/4)^(1/4). The full four-torsion field of E3:y²=x³+1 is Q(i,r), and this is also the field of all x-coordinates of E4[3], where E4:y²=x³−x. Explicit inverse algebraic formulas prove both equalities. On E3 the point Q0=(0,1) has order3. For P∈E3[4], the identity y(P)=x(P)²[x(P−Q0)−x(P+Q0)]/4 recovers the full four-torsion field from the projective twelve-torsion field. This is the exact reason the single global sign matters: separate projective E3[4] gives only Q(ζ12), while projective E3[12] contains the full nonabelian field.

Let B=C2*C3 and Q=SL2(Z/12)/global±1, of order576. Its torsion-free kernel N has rank97. Over L, every E0 four-torsion unit of norm1 is uniquely τ ε, where τ is its order-three lift of the mod2 unit and ε=±I. The cubic Kummer formula identifies ε with the actual full E3[4] action. ### D_FERMAT_JOINT

# D.37: an actual mixed Fermat12 shadow and its two compact cover actions

The new result is a fully generating finite GT0 shadow on a quotient that contains the congruence data and the common Fermat12 homology. Its actual normalized cubic-cover Tate4 matrix is -I, and its actual quartic-cover Tate3 matrix is I. Both are obtained from the same cyclic12 compact action through the exact maps of D36. ## Exact quotient and source maps

Let B=C2*C3, F=ker(B→S3), Q=SL2(Z/12)/{±I}, N=ker(B→Q), and F12=ker(F→(Z/12)^2), with the positive loops x,y mapping to the ordered basis. Set L=N∩F12. The explicit affine representation sends s to the swap matrix with zero translation and r to the order-three matrix (0,-1;1,-1) with translation e1. Direct multiplication gives x=t^-2 the translation e1 and y=sxs the translation e2, so its kernel is exactly F'. Joining its mod12 reduction with Q gives H=B/L of order6912.

The kernel of H→Q is the twelve-element subgroup {(a,b):a,b even,a+b=0 mod3}, isomorphic to C2²×C3. The corresponding graph has6912 edges,5760 vertices and1153 fundamental cycles. D.24 neither proves nor refutes the existence of such a different lift.

### D_PERFECT_COMPLETION

# D40: all perfect source quotients and an exact symmetric extension

## Result and remaining scope

D40 extends the normalized D39 action through **every finite perfect quotient of the full marked source** \(B=C_2*C_3\), simultaneously and compatibly. This includes every good-prime congruence quotient \(\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb Z/p^e)\), for every \(p\ge5\) and every \(e\). It also constructs an actual further mixed extension by a marked \(S_7\) quotient.

Let \(\mathcal R\) be the closure of the diagonal image of \(B\) in the product of all its finite perfect quotients. The theorem proves the exact common quotient

\[
\mathcal R^{\mathrm{ab}}=C_6=B^{\mathrm{ab}},
\]

with the original marked characters of \(s\) and \(r\). The source images are exactly

\[
\mathcal U=\mathcal T\times_{C_6}\mathcal R,
\qquad
\mathcal V=\mathcal U\times_{C_2}S_7.
\]

The automorphisms are \((\alpha_{39},1)\) and \((\alpha_{39},1,1)\). The proof constructs the simultaneous loop conjugators and the common profinite gauge. It verifies the actual zero images of the D39 normalized conjugators in \(B^{\mathrm{ab}}\), so the fibre-product compatibility is pointwise.

The D.45 excludes a full profinite lift of this recorded action. Its exact remaining kernel is

\[
R_T\cap K_{\mathcal R}\cap\ker\sigma_7.
\]

Under a separately evaluated marked \(S_{10}\) quotient, this kernel maps onto \(A_{10}\). This proves a nontrivial remaining source layer. It is not a theorem that the whole CM pair cannot lift.

## Structural proof

For a finite marked subdirect image \(H\subset\prod_iG_i\), with every \(G_i\) perfect, its stable derived subgroup \(H_\infty\) projects onto every coordinate. Consequently every coordinate kernel maps onto \(H/H_\infty\). An iterated commutator of all coordinate kernels is trivial because it is contained in their zero intersection. Thus \(H/H_\infty\) is nilpotent.

A finite nilpotent quotient of \(C_2*C_3\) is generated by commuting elements of orders dividing two and three, and is cyclic of order dividing six. Hence \(H'\) is perfect, and the maximal prosolvable quotient of \(\mathcal R\) divides \(C_6\).

Two explicit perfect central quotients determine both factors exactly:

| Quotient | Exact order | Independent orbit lengths | Central twist |
|---|---:|---|---|
| \(\operatorname{SL}_3(\mathbb F_7)\) | 5,630,688 | 342, 336, 49 | Replace \(r\) by \(2Ir\), giving \(C_3\). |
| \(\operatorname{SL}_4(\mathbb F_3)\) | 12,130,560 | 80, 78, 72, 27 | Replace \(s\) by \(-Is\), giving \(C_2\). |

Every generator is an explicit matrix in the theorem. The original and twisted pairs satisfy \(s^2=r^3=1\) and generate the full displayed groups. The elementary identity \([E_{ij}(a),E_{jk}(b)]=E_{ik}(ab)\), together with row elimination, proves perfectness. The twists agree with the original maps on \(B'\), so their joint images supply the exact central characters of \(B^{\mathrm{ab}}\). No external perfect-group classification is used.

D39's \(\mathcal T\) is prosolvable, and retains \(B^{\mathrm{ab}}\). The two maps therefore have maximal common marked quotient exactly \(C_6\). The closed subdirect-product argument identifies the actual source image with the fibre product, in both directions.

## Pointwise conjugators and gauge

The raw D39 conjugators have retained affine coordinates \((6,0)\), and the common gauge \(k\) is zero modulo six. The exact map from the \(x,y\) abelianization of \(F\) to \(B^{\mathrm{ab}}=C_2\times C_3\) sends \((a,b)\) to \((0,a+b\bmod3)\). Thus the raw and normalized D39 conjugators all have zero \(B\)-abelianization.

prove perfectness over every \(\mathbb Z/p^e\), \(p\ge5\). The commutator convention is explicitly \(aba^{-1}b^{-1}\). ## Proved result and exact source

The actual D.49 action extends across its entire remaining two-adic homology kernel over the actual group \(G_3\), whose order is \(576\cdot3^{97}\). The proof uses the same actual arithmetic element \(h\), its parameter \(f_h\), and its nonzero normal elementary three-subgroup \(A\). It does not substitute a finite guess for that parameter.

For \(K_3=\ker(\widehat B\to G_3)\), put \(U=K_3^{\mathrm{ab},(2)}\) and \(U^-=(1-e_A)U\). Its rank is
\[
 w=\frac{|G_3|}{6}\left(1-\frac1{|A|}\right)\ge |G_3|/9>0.
\]
The exact image of the old full-source kernel in the complete two-adic homology extension is \(U^-\). The exact common marked quotient is consequently \(\mathcal J_\infty/U^-\), and the new actual source is
\[
 \widehat B/(\mathfrak R_h\cap\mathfrak K_\infty)
 =\mathcal W_h\times_{\mathcal J_\infty/U^-}\mathcal J_\infty.
\]
Its projection onto the complete D.49 source has kernel \(U^-\). Both projection kernels and the full kernel from \(\widehat B\) are recorded in (D.50.5). Every previously retained action, normalized parameter and comparison is preserved through this literal projection.

## Initial repair includes generation

The first cusp correction is onto because the moving module has zero \(G_3\)-coinvariants. This first step gives a peripheral endomorphism; generation is then proved by a separate explicit repair.

The proof specifies both vertex-module bijections and the complete edge chain map in the actual Bass–Serre graph. The displayed path identity verifies that its restriction is the actual homology action, including the basepoint transport. The nilpotent Fitting part of the edge map lies in the moving cycle module and is a direct summand of the regular group module. It is therefore projective.

The regular-module projection composed with the path cocycle is an explicit derivation. The full inflation–restriction comparison is included; the proof does not leave the needed class unspecified. The exact cusp order is 36. The fixed cusp cycle has zero projection onto the nilpotent Fitting part, so the derivation's cusp value has zero cyclic norm. Projectivity after restriction to the cusp cyclic group gives an actual solution of the norm equation without division by 36. The resulting inner-derivation correction preserves the exact cusp and changes the two conjugators by specified elements of the moving kernel.

On homology the repaired map is \(T+e\): it is \(T\) on the invertible part and \(1+T\) on the nilpotent part. This proves generation and hence an automorphism at the first level.

## Exact higher common quotients and inverse limit

At level \(2^k\), the already retained joint source has common marked quotient
\[
 \mathcal J_k/(2^{k-1}W_k)
 =\mathcal J_{k-1}\times_{\mathcal J_{k-1}/W_{k-1}}
                    (\mathcal J_k/W_k).
\]
This follows from the original source kernels and their preimages; no unsupported interchange of images and intersections is used.

The lower and D.49 conjugators agree as elements in the common quotient. They lift simultaneously, and a correction in \(2^{k-1}W_k\) kills the exact cusp error. The unchanged invertible reduction modulo two proves generation by the determinant criterion over \(\mathbb Z/2^k\). Thus the actual conjugators, automorphisms and inverses form compatible systems. Their inverse limit proves the claimed complete two-adic lift.

The retained source contains the full loop abelianization and has kernel inside \(\widehat F''\). Consequently the new parameter has an actual \(\widehat F'\) preimage, and the full parameter and CM normalization descend exactly to D.49.

## Explicit next remaining finite kernel

The full-source kernel is still nonzero. The next finite quotient is the actual class-two exponent-four quotient of \(K_3\), with abelianization \((\mathbb Z/4)^\rho\) and commutator module \(\bigwedge^2_{\mathbb F_2}(U/2U)\). Actual preimages \(z_i\in\mathfrak R_h\) of a basis of \(U^-\), and \(x_j\in K_3\) of a basis of \(U\), give

This is an explicit inclusion with actual source elements. The theorem does not claim equality with the whole remaining image or a lift through this next quotient. Full profinite GT0 realization remains unresolved.

The initial central coordinates can be changed while retaining the original homology masks, right-centralizer data and G3 conjugators. ### D_RELATIVE_PRONILPOTENT

# D42: lifting the evaluated projective-linear residual through its full pronilpotent kernel

## Proved result

D42 extends the actual D41 action through every relative pro-p completion of the recorded PGL2(F7) source quotient, simultaneously for all primes. This is a completed extension of one specific remaining finite quotient through its entire pronilpotent kernel. The source is the proved fibre product of W and P_J,nil over the explicit common quotient C54. Its action is (alpha41, identity), with actual simultaneous peripheral conjugators and the unchanged D39 global gauge. Every earlier compact-cover matrix, normalized G6 action and fixed-fibre CM pair remains unchanged under the specified projections.

The comparison quotient C54 is the inverse limit of B/[L54,L54]L54^(3^n). The actual finite base H54 has54 elements, and the kernel at the completed level is Z3^10. Every finite level has order54 times3^(10n), with cusp order6 times3^n. The finite base map is literally (q,a,b) to (q O2(Q),a mod3,b mod3), on the previous6912 states. Its128-element kernel is O2(H).

The even subgroup of H54 has coordinates (u,v,w) in F3^3, with multiplication (u+u',v+v',w+w'+uv'). The actual source map sends r to(1,0,0), srs to(0,1,0), and the commutator to(0,0,1). Conjugation by s sends (u,v,w) to(v,u,uv-w). These formulas identify the marking as well as the abstract order54 group.

## Why this is the exact common quotient

Every finite quotient of the new relative pronilpotent source that retains J has a nilpotent kernel V over J. In a nonsolvable further quotient, the image of V can be divided out to give J itself: the other two possible normal images in J lead to solvable quotients. Since W has no J quotient, every common finite image is solvable.

The image of the even source C3*C3 in such a solvable quotient is an image of V after its perfect residual is killed. It is therefore nilpotent and generated by two elements of order3, hence a3-group. This computes the full maximal solvable quotient as the relative pro3 completion of B over the sign C2. It also shows why the only nontrivial prime in the comparison is3.

The properfect kernel of W to T dies in every solvable image. The finite2 kernel of T to Hhat dies in every relative pro3 even image. Finally L54/L has order128, so the closed images of L and L54 in such a target coincide. The first is abelian; therefore the second is abelian. This forces the map through C54. The reverse maps are supplied by the actual source definitions. Closed Goursat and compactness then give the full fibre-product image, not just an inclusion.

## The nonzero conjugator is retained

The projected normalized D39 A is1. Its beta is the nonzero fixed vector -(e7+e8)/2 in the completed rank10 homology kernel. Pairing this conjugator with1 would fail, so D42 does not make that substitution.

Instead P_J,3 maps onto J times_C2 C54 with a pro3 kernel. The pair (1,beta_C) centralizes the image of the involution s. The proof lifts it to a genuine centralizer of s by conjugating two involutions through that pro3 kernel. The finite argument is an induction on the centre of a3-group, with its final correction z^(-1/2); compactness gives one coherent element for all finite levels. Its J coordinate remains1. At every other prime the conjugator can be1, so these choices form one element of the full pronilpotent fibre product.

The resulting f is exactly (f39,1). It has an actual Fhat-prime preimage: the W coordinate already retains the entire abelianization of Fhat and has zero coordinate there. Thus the peripheral equations, both multiplier-one hexagons, and generation are proved on the actual completed source quotient. Generation follows from the invertible product automorphism on the exact fibre product.

The finite joint source map to PGL2(F7) times_C2 H54 has9072 elements. Its kernel M has rank1513 and index27 in N_J. ## Main corrections and proofs

1. The affine rank-one map is the Kummer action on compatible roots of −1/2. Its image is the parity graph of the quadratic character χ−8, of index two. On the cyclotomic kernel the rational-prime tuple has image exactly 2 Zhat^r, not all Zhat^r. A short real-conjugate argument computes every radical relation and a perfect finite Kummer pairing gives the exact image.
2. The general openness question of scan166 is proved directly. A uniform Sah cocycle argument forces a bounded power of every cyclotomically divisible element to be divisible already in the ground field. Finitely generated units in a normal arithmetic model then bound all Kummer indices uniformly. The full-unit-group and saturated surjectivity suggestions fail for Spec Z[1/2][t].
3. The local inertia image is also exact, not only open. Odd-ℓ indices are ℓ^(vℓ(p^(ℓ−1)−1)−1) for p≠ℓ and one for p=ℓ. At two they are two for p=2 and 2^(v2(p²−1)−2) for odd p.
4. The Fermat Jacobi computation keeps the manuscript's plus model. The twist factor is θ(−1)^(a+b). At p43,n6 the two traces are32 and−64, so ignoring the arithmetic descent would give a wrong result.
5. Soulé certificates at q≡1 modℓ² give nonzero modℓ values for every requested character. Their distinct cyclotomic eigenspaces ensure that the classes do not disappear in the infinite ℓ-cyclotomic extension. At37 every weight3,5,…,35 is witnessed at5477. The separate Bernoulli calculation finds(37,32), which concerns the minus class group and does not imply a Vandiver gap.
6. The exact metabelian image ranks of all special derivations in weights1…10 are0,0,1,0,3,0,5,0,7,0; invariant ranks are0,0,1,0,1,0,1,0,2,0. The extra invariant weight7 direction dies. The pro-ℓ successive-approximation claim from LM3 was independently proved, including the convergence and automorphism steps. Coprime fixed-point lifting handles S3 forℓ≥5.
7. The two normalizer coordinates have a direct modular-stack interpretation. The quotient [M0,4/S3] is the rigidification of M1,1 by±1; the cocycle/path comparison gives the projective Tate representations of x³+1 and x³−x. The projective torsion fields are K3^ab and K4^ab. Their intersection is exactly Q^ab Q(i,3^(1/4)): two abelian index2 subgroups force a commutator group of order at most2, and the explicit D4 extension realizes it. Thus the joint image has index2 in equal norms.
8. The local paired norms have one quadratic obstruction at each of2 and3. Finite enumeration verifies index2 from mod4 at2 and mod3 at3. Global roots identify their diagonal, leaving the single global relation above.
9. The cusp Tate parameter is q=λ²/256 times a rational power-series unit of constant1. The positive tangential section fixes λ roots and those unit roots, hence the genuine normalized matrix is [[χ,−8κ2],[0,1]]. Positive real q roots and the branch-cut period ratio fix the otherwise possible shear. This agrees with agent A's independent division-polynomial computation of the mod12 image of order12.
10. The original Ihara sequel gives phi_n(f_g)=y^(−kappa_n(g))f_g, with phi_n induced by the n-th power cover followed by filling all root-of-unity punctures except1. Its Introduction explicitly attaches the beta/gamma factorization to the pentagon equation. Its Supplement corrects the Jacobi indices in Ihara1986 Theorem7, exponent signs in Theorem10, and an inversion in Anderson1989 p125.
11. For a leading special term f=p(X,Y)[x,y], the exact Fox map is M(f)=XYp and the derived-module action is −XYp. Gamma factorization restricts this to a scalar times X^n+Y^n−(X+Y)^n. The special condition eliminates even n. E.3 provides actual Galois nonzero classes for everyℓ, so actual Galois and full GT both have ranks0,0,1,0,1,0,1,0,1,0 through10. The weight9 extra S3 vector fails this gamma condition.
12. Dolgushev–Guynee2024 Theorem5.2/Corollary5.4/Proposition5.5 gives the exact remaining profinite test: a finite GT0 shadow comes from a genuine element exactly when it lifts through every finer finite B3 quotient. This is stronger than lifting through pro-ℓ nilpotent quotients. The criterion is recorded as D.22 with its compactness proof.
13. Write the Ihara conjugator in the derived module as u_g[x,y]. Exact commutator calculus gives B_g=1+(s−1)(t−1)u_g and F_g=A_χ(s)A_χ(t)B_g. Reducing u_g at t=1,s^n=1, the Kummer character κ_n is coefficient0 minus coefficient(n−1). This proves the proposed full profinite comparison positively. For oddℓ, pro-ℓ tame inertia at2 fixes every root of unity and acts trivially on the normalized pro-ℓ fundamental group, but κ2,ℓ is onto. Hence retaining full χ still does not recover this Kummer datum from the pure pro-ℓ series.
14. The common nonabelian finite CM field is Q(i,(3/4)^(1/4)): full E3[4] and projective E4[3] are equal to it. Projective E3[12] recovers full4 by the rational three-torsion point's sign. A finite refinement with quotient of order576·6^97 realizes twelve extra-CM-coset automorphisms, with full-rank97 checks at both2 and3. A precise cusp gauge makes these actual finiteGT0 shadows; their incompatible restrictions to the common field prove nonarithmeticality. No full profinite lift is inferred.

# D.34–D.35: the actual elliptic cover and its automorphism source

These two results give exact structural maps for the remaining relative pro-two lifting problem. D.34 identifies the retained rank-four group with the three-punctured genus-one curve w³=z(z−1), including its positive Γ(2) monodromy, all eighteen deck transformations, all three cusp words, and all 54 cusp conjugacy maps. D.35 proves actual odd-deck equivariant boundary lifts and then proves that the whole compact CM torus is already realized by genuine Galois action centralizing every deck transformation. D45 proves the recorded finite-action fibre empty. The prescribed CM pair remains open for a replacement action.

## Exact geometric and group maps

The regular Q0 dessin has cycles 2^9, 3^6, and 6^3. It has genus one and three punctures. The explicit deck maps are δ(z,w)=(z,ζw), s(z,w)=(1−z,w), and r=δr0 with r0(z,w)=(1/(1−z),−w/(1−z)). The standard positive generators x=(sr)^−2 and y=sxs both act by δ, so the cyclic-three cover is identified by its actual monodromy character. The normalized invariant map is 4(z²−z+1)³/(27z²(z−1)²).

The fixed K basis gives cusp words a1 a4^−1 a2, a1^−1 a3 a2^−1, and a3^−1 a4. ## The decisive vanishing argument

In a finite common quotient Q, the image P of R-prime times the alternating product is exactly Q's perfect residual. The isolated T-prime coordinate has image Z that centralizes P. Its even image modulo the nilpotent base kernel centralizes a centreless product of simple groups, so Z lies in that nilpotent kernel.

For p other than3, the Sylow Z_p lies in P and is central there, because the quotient Q/P has pro3 even part. Every even element acts on Z_p through the nilpotent kernel, hence through a p-group. The actual even source is generated by r and srs of order3, so the action is trivial.

The final map is only the restriction B-prime to T-prime, followed by the isolated-coordinate and Sylow maps. There is no projection of all B_even onto T-prime. Its kernel is normal in B_even by the established invariance. The resulting quotient is a central extension of B_even/B-prime=C3 generated by those two order3 elements. It is abelian of exponent3, so its p-not3 image vanishes. Thus Z is a3-group, and the isolated kernel of T to C54 dies in every common quotient.

The exact common-kernel identity follows by combining this proved inclusion with the modular law for closed normal subgroups:

    R_W R_family = (K_C54 intersect K_D) R_family

Both source maps retain the original C6, which gives the stated common fibre product.

# D41: all actual symmetric quotients

## Proved result

D41 extends D40 simultaneously through every actual epimorphism from the marked source \(B=C_2*C_3\) onto a full symmetric group \(S_n\), \(n\ge5\). One coordinate is indexed by each distinct marked kernel. The exact simultaneous image is the full fibre product over the common sign:

\[
\mathcal S=\{(g_M):\operatorname{sign}(g_M)\text{ is the same in every coordinate}\}.
\]

Its even subgroup is the full product \(\prod_M A_M\), and the actual image of \(s\) gives a section of the sign map. Thus the theorem specifies both maps in the semidirect-product description and the action of the section.

The source image jointly with D40's \(\mathcal U\) is exactly

\[
\mathcal W=\mathcal U\times_{C_2}\mathcal S
=\widehat B/(R_T\cap K_{\mathcal R}\cap K_{\mathcal S}).
\]

The normalized action is \((\alpha_{39},1_{\mathcal R},1_{\mathcal S})\), with actual simultaneous conjugators, one global profinite gauge, multiplier one and both hexagons. It extends the D40 action exactly and now includes identity on the explicitly marked \(S_{10}\) quotient.

## Exact source and quotient proofs

Every symmetric epimorphism has the same sign character: \(r\) is even because its order divides three, and \(s\) must be odd for surjectivity. Restricting to \(B_{\rm even}\) gives epimorphisms onto the alternating factors with exactly the original marked kernels. These kernels are distinct.

Inductive simple-group Goursat proves surjectivity onto every finite product of alternating factors. A nontrivial common quotient would identify one new kernel with an old one. Compactness then gives the full infinite product. Multiplication by the actual \(s\) tuple supplies the odd coset.

Repeated epimorphisms with the same kernel are compared by the induced coordinate automorphism. Deletion and reinsertion through that automorphism give explicit inverse marked maps. Their even images are diagonal simple blocks, whose normalizers inside the corresponding alternating products are the blocks themselves.

A proper normal subgroup of a finite simultaneous symmetric image lies in its even subgroup. An odd element in a normal subgroup would act nontrivially on every surviving alternating factor, forcing all those factors into the subgroup. Therefore every nonabelian finite quotient of the simultaneous image has a full symmetric quotient. The iterated-coordinate-kernel argument of D40 excludes every such symmetric quotient of \(\mathcal U\), so their exact common quotient is sign \(C_2\).

The exact projection kernels are

\[
\ker(\mathcal W\to\mathcal V)=\prod_{M\ne M_7}A_M,
\qquad
\ker(\mathcal W\to\mathcal T)=\mathcal R'\times\prod_M A_M.
\]

Here \(\mathcal V\) is D40's specified \(S_7\) extension, and \(\mathcal R'\) retains its closed-commutator definition.

## New remaining-kernel test

The program evaluates the actual source map

\[
s\mapsto\left[\begin{smallmatrix}1&0\\0&-1\end{smallmatrix}\right],
\qquad
r\mapsto\left[\begin{smallmatrix}1&-1\\1&0\end{smallmatrix}\right]
\]

to \(J=\operatorname{PGL}_2(\mathbb F_7)\). It generates all 336 elements. Its determinant-square subgroup \(L=\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb F_7)\) has order 168. The even-source generators \(r,srs\) generate \(L\).

The certificate exhausts all six conjugacy classes in \(L\), of sizes \(1,21,42,56,24,24\). Every nonidentity class normally generates all of \(L\). The centralizer of \(L\) in \(J\) has order one. These direct checks give the simplicity and normal-subgroup inputs used in the proof.

The action on \(\mathbb P^1(\mathbb F_7)\) is faithful of degree eight and has image order 336. It is therefore not a surjection onto \(S_8\), whose order is 40320. This specific map is not one of the full symmetric coordinates.

An iterated-coordinate-kernel argument proves that \(\mathcal W\) has no quotient \(J\): the solvable-coordinate and symmetric-coordinate kernels have images containing \(L\), while the perfect-coordinate kernels have image \(J\). Their iterated commutator cannot simultaneously be trivial in the product and contain the perfect group \(L\).

Consequently

\[
\sigma(R_T\cap K_{\mathcal R}\cap K_{\mathcal S})=L.
\]

This is the exact remaining source-kernel map. It does not assert that the entire CM pair has no full profinite lift.

## Comparison of the two mod-seven markings

The canonical good-prime map uses the previously fixed matrices

\[
\rho_7(s)=\left[\begin{smallmatrix}0&1\\-1&0\end{smallmatrix}\right],
\qquad
\rho_7(r)=\left[\begin{smallmatrix}0&-1\\1&-1\end{smallmatrix}\right].
\]

The program enumerates all 56,448 paired elements under \((\rho_7,\sigma)\), proving the full joint image \(L\times J\). On the actual even source it enumerates all 28,224 pairs, proving image \(L^2\). Thus the comparison is through explicit marked maps.

The new remaining kernel kills the canonical \(\rho_7\) coordinate, which belongs to the all-perfect construction, while it surjects under the displayed second map onto \(L\). The finite pair calculation and the full-kernel proof are stated separately.

Nakamura's original modular construction identifies the action on the Tate kernel with conjugation by c^E, whose augmentation is the discriminant Kummer cocycle divided by twelve. ### E_FIRST_THREE

# E.49: complete global H sections with first Kummer class three

The fixed raw first cocycle is A=3 kappa_3(3), using positive compatible real roots. The result evaluates its entire global section fiber in the actual arithmetic quotient H.

Let P be the rational primes p!=3 for which the residue of 27 has multiplicative order prime to three. The complete coordinates are

Z_3 × R(P) × Z_3 × Z_3[[Z]],

where R(P) is the module of Z_3 sequences tending to zero modulo every power of three. The scaled toric coordinate is

(kappa_3(3), b_3 kappa_3(3) + sum_p b_p kappa_3(p)).

Thus the toric image is explicitly determined, including all completed inputs. Every toric fiber has the compact coordinates Z_3 × Z_3[[Z]]. All these parameter groups are torsion-free.

## Actual arithmetic normal form

The proof changes the prolongations of the two actual Frobenii over seven by an explicit conjugation. If the initial Kummer value is t=1 mod3, conjugation by rho_0^((t^-1-1)/12) changes it to one. The conjugate generator has value minus one, and their Frattini classes remain the free basis.

With sigma=tau and rho=tau' tau^-1, the twisted coefficient action is exactly

sigma(u)=7T^3 u(T^7), rho(u)=T^-6 u, C(u)=-u(T^-1).

Every unramified section class has the unique representative

v(tau)=3b_3 + z eta + T^3 W f(W^2),

where W=(T-T^-1)/2 and eta=1-(T^-1+T^-2)/2. The proof gives the correcting coboundary by division by T^-6-1, proves that this correction is C-fixed, and extracts the remaining odd W-series. The division is integral because (T^-6-1) and (T^3-1) generate the same ideal. Every inverse map is continuous on its actual closed principal ideal.

## Finite coefficients and residue surjectivity

For the actual finite coefficient images, the action over K=Q(zeta_3) has three-power image. Degree-one cocycles factor through the maximal pro-three quotient by their graph homomorphisms. Higher full-group cohomology vanishes by the proved trivial-F_3 composition series and the arithmetic dimension bound. This supplies the precise finite-coefficient comparison with the free group.

At p!=3, removing the Tate twist gives the affine Frobenius x -> A_F+p x. Its unique possible fixed point is integral exactly when ord_p(27) is prime to three. In that case the invariant integral measure lattice is Z_3 delta_x; otherwise it is zero. Nonfixed affine orbits admit no nonzero invariant integral measure, as their successive orbit partitions force arbitrarily high three-divisibility. Intersecting with J gives 3 Z_3 delta_x, whose scaled toric residue is integral.

Finite localization yields a short exact degree-one sequence because the unramified degree-two group is zero. The left finite groups are Mittag-Leffler. Its inverse limit is therefore exactly onto the restricted residue families, without a closure or an omitted inverse-limit term. All primes p=2 mod3 are allowed, including two.

## Independent field and norm calculation

The actual finite etale algebras factor into

F_n=Q(3^(1/3^(n-1))) and L_n=Q(3^(1/3^(n-1)) zeta_(3^n)).

The degrees are 3^(n-1) and 2*3^(n-1). The lambda-valuations 2/3^(n-1) prove the full local degrees over K and total ramification at the unique prime above three. The L_n tower and the auxiliary KF_n tower are cyclic cubic at each step and ramify nowhere else.

The ambiguous class formula proves that their class numbers are prime to three. The strongly ambiguous formula then makes the ordinary-unit norm index divide a prime-to-three class number; since that index is a power of three, it is one. Thus actual ordinary-unit norms are onto in the cyclic towers. Averaging complex conjugation by (1+c)/2 gives surjective completed unit norms in the F_n tower.

The ordinary-unit norm limit is completely evaluated by the power series f. At level one, Shapiro and the exact Kummer relation lambda^2=-3 zeta_3 identify the two three-prime valuations of a+z eta+T^3Wf as (a+z,-z). Their vanishing leaves exactly T^3Wf. This proves the entire ordinary-unit limit, not only a rank bound.

For p!=3, finite Frobenius orbits are the prime ideals of the etale algebra; pushing their orbit sums multiplies by the relative residue degree, exactly the ideal norm. The unique integral affine fixed point gives a compatible degree-one prime chain. Every other valuation disappears. The full valuation limit is Z_3^2 × R(P).

For each allowed prime, completed principal divisors exist because the class groups are prime to three. Surjective unit norms then correct chosen generators recursively into a coherent system pi_p. The specified three-unit systems xi_F,xi_L and ordinary-unit system epsilon(f) give every section through

epsilon(f) xi_F^(3b_3+z) xi_L^(-z) product_p pi_p^(3b_p).

The cube condition at level zero is exactly the stated divisibility of the three-prime sum and the other valuation coefficients. This independently constructs every allowed global class by actual norm equations.

### E_FRATTINI

# E.59 — Actual inert Frobenius, its Frattini orbit, and exact rank densities

Status: Proved. Independent full-proof and fresh-execution audit PASS, retained in ledger/a_independent_audits.md. The auditor rebuilt the certified field and reran both finite-field and fixed-module certificates with identical JSON.

## Exact arithmetic outcome

The arithmetic group is the actual free pro-three group P=G_(Q(zeta3),{3})^(3), with the c-equivariant basis from E.55. Its Frattini field is Q(zeta9,cuberoot3). Every inert rational prime ell congruent to8 modulo9 has its arithmetic Frobenius in D3(P). The entire image in D3/D4 is

    x^(3 alpha_ell) [y,[x,y]]^beta_ell,

where alpha_ell=(ell^2-1)/9 modulo3, and beta_ell is the cubic residue of the explicit cross ratio

    h=(r-u)(omega^2 r-u1)/((omega^2 r-u)(r-u1)),
    u=-z^2(1-z)^2, u1=-z^8(1-z^4)^2,
    z=zeta9, omega=z^3, r^3=3.

The proof supplies the actual Kummer morphism and its sign. The exact equality ((3-u^3)/(3-u1^3))*gamma=9q^3 gives (Y^-1 h)/h=gamma times an explicit cube. The eigenspace argument proves gamma is a noncube before the associated Heisenberg extension is used. That extension pairs gamma with [x,y] as1. At the prime over two, h reduces to omega^2 in F64 and has trivial cubic residue, so h is precisely the second dual coordinate. The other dual coordinate is zeta9. This evaluates the word without presuming a presentation of a larger arithmetic group.

At17 the formula gives x^6[y,[x,y]]^2. The Frobenius has exact first Frattini depth and exact Zassenhaus degree three. Its nine conjugates have rank five in the ten-dimensional Frattini quotient. Each is nonzero, but their exact span prevents their being included simultaneously in a free generating set.

## Full finite module and density calculation

Continuous Fox derivatives give the actual module

    Phi(P)/Phi(Phi(P)) = ker(R^2 -> R), R=F3[s,t]/(s^3,t^3),
    boundary(A,B)=sA+tB.

Complex conjugation acts by substitution Y->Y^-1 and the derivative factor -Y^-1 on the second coordinate. Its fixed space has basis a=(s^2,0), b=(-t^2,st-st^2), sb,s^2b. The cyclic submodules of all81 fixed vectors have ranks0,1,3,5 with counts1,2,24,54. Every case, including zero coordinates, is proved by explicit independent generators and annihilating polynomials.

The finite arithmetic quotient P/Phi(Phi(P)) is the full Fox group of pairs(q,D) with boundaryD=q-1, of order3^12. The exact chi9=-1 subset in its c-extension consists of q=Y^b. Squaring and then multiplying byY^b gives the surjection (1+c)Y^b from the ten-dimensional cycle space to its four-dimensional fixed subspace. Its kernel has dimension six. Consequently every fixed vector has3^7 preimages across the three values ofb. Chebotarev applied to these actual conjugacy-stable subsets gives relative Dirichlet densities1/81,2/81,8/27,2/3 among primes ell=8 mod9, and absolute densities1/486,1/243,4/81,1/9.

This density proof uses the entire finite Galois quotient. It is independent of the finite sample of prime residues.

### E_GLOBAL_GLUING

# E.73 — exact global presentation and coupled local packets

## Result

The actual maximal pro-three arithmetic group over Q(zeta3) has a minimal convergent presentation with two chosen unramified generators and one inertia generator for every prime away from three. The full closed relation subgroup is generated by the actual local words

`Y_q T_q Y_q^-1 T_q^(-Nq)`.

Here `Y_q` is a chosen preimage of the actual local Frobenius in that free group. The proof does not set it equal to the word of its image in the unramified quotient.

Consequently a section over the free unramified quotient, together with a null family of inertia values, extends globally exactly when evaluation of all those actual words gives the local tame relations. These equations are sufficient. The local class spaces already computed in E.61, E.66, E.68 and E.71 are their exact packets. The complete geometric conjugation action, the comparison to the two intrinsic quotients, and rational descent by actual complex-conjugation words are included.

The equations retain their arithmetic coupling. Their proof does not evaluate every higher Frobenius word or solve every coupled global system. Those remain the specific arithmetic inputs for the outstanding global classification.

## Exact support and relation-module proof

The inertia-bundle universal property identifies H1 of its kernel with a direct sum over base primes of locally constant coinduced modules on the prolongation spaces. Continuity forces finite support in the base primes. Since the unramified group is free on two generators, its H1 commutes with this direct sum. The explicit Shapiro cochain and the central-extension edge map identify global H2 with the direct sum of the actual local H2 groups.

The dual relation module is a compact product. The displayed local relators evaluate against its dual classes as the coordinate vectors. Their closed normal closure is the entire kernel: a surviving nontrivial normal pro-three subgroup would have a finite quotient with a nonzero invariant character, contradicting that spanning property. Inertia generators converge because every finite extension ramifies at only finitely many primes. The relators then converge regardless of the chosen Frobenius preimages.

The source/module maps and all conjugation signs are written in the proof. In particular the positive local relation error agrees with evaluation of the coinduced cocycle at the base inertia generator.

# E.47: the entire zero-first-coordinate global family

The exact target is the arithmetic group H = 3 Z_3 semidirect (3,U) Z_3[[U]], with the genuine action proved in E.38. The global section classes whose first class is zero are completely evaluated as

## Exact maps and proof

The first cocycle is normalized uniquely by the geometric first coordinate s=-a(c)/2. Residual conjugation is precisely a coboundary in J=(3,U)Z_3[[U]]. The Galois-module isomorphism sends a measure to its augmentation and its restrictions to the annuli 3^j Z_3^times. Its inverse is the centered series

nu delta_0 + sum_j (mu_j - aug(mu_j) delta_0).

At positional level Z/3^k, every term with j>=k is zero. This proves actual continuity and explains why unrestricted products, with no convergence condition on their coordinates, occur.

Finite Shapiro evaluation at the position one identifies an annular class with Kummer classes of K_n=Q(zeta_(3^n)); the positional pushforward is actual corestriction and hence the field norm. The Shapiro identity explicitly proves that natural field Galois action corresponds to inverse deck translation. This is retained in both the root-of-unity line and the group-ring coordinates.

Every norm-coherent multiplicative class has zero valuations away from 3: after finitely many layers, each fixed prime has residue degree multiplied by 3 at every step, forcing each compatible valuation into all powers of 3. The kernel of all completed valuations is exactly the completion of the 3-units, proved using the free principal-divisor image and the prime-to-3 S-class group.

The 3-primary class group of every K_n vanishes. Lemmermeyer's opened Theorem 1 applies with a single totally ramified prime and no infinite ramification. Its ambiguous class number has no factor 3 by induction from the Eisenstein PID. A nonzero finite 3-group under a group of order 3 has nonzero invariants. The real subfield's 3-primary class group injects into the complex one because ideal extension followed by norm is multiplication by 2.

The opened proof of Sharifi's Theorem 4.3.3 gives the exact real circular-unit index and normalized independent generators. For epsilon_(n,a)=(1-zeta_n^a)(1-zeta_n^-a), the exact relation epsilon_(n,a)/epsilon_n = eta_(n,a)^2 shows that the subgroup of real 3-units generated by all epsilon conjugates has index 2^(3^(n-1)-1) h(K_n^+), prime to 3. These conjugates therefore give a basis of the completed real 3-units. The minus part of the completed complex 3-units is exactly mu_(3^n), by the quotient u/conj(u) and the elementary root-of-unity criterion. Thus the finite completed 3-unit group is actually mu_(3^n) + Z_3[Gamma_n].

Norm sends zeta_(n+1) to zeta_n and epsilon_(n+1) to epsilon_n. On their exponents these are reduction and group-ring projection, respectively. This evaluates the norm limit as Z_3(1) + Z_3[[Gamma]], with explicit generators at every level.

## Proved criterion

The criterion includes the continuous inverse-limit obstruction. The support sequence is formed at finite coefficients, with direct sums over finite sets of removed primes, before taking the derived inverse limit. The actual image, without closure, occurs in the resulting restricted-sequence cokernel.

## Exact maps and gluing

The actual lower cocycle and a continuous Hall set lift give a continuous central two-cocycle. On tame inertia, the homomorphism t^s -> n^s is an explicit local splitting. Its Frobenius defect, divided by q, is the chain-level localization representative. Changing inertia lifts gives precisely the differential q^(-1)Frobenius-1. Central changes of Frobenius lifts have no effect.

At each finite coefficient level, the local splitting cochains vanish outside finitely many base primes uniformly over prolongations. Their dependence on conjugating elements is locally constant. Global localization gives a lift, and its degree-one residue sequence adjusts the lift to the prescribed inertia. The remaining choices are finite unramified cocycles. Compactness gives compatible continuous global lifts. This establishes actual gluing and the continuous inertia-bundle equivariance, rather than assuming that arbitrary pointwise inertia lifts already glue.

Restriction to K detects the rational obstruction because restriction followed by corestriction is multiplication2. Every nonempty rational geometric-conjugacy fiber is the explicit matrix-cokernel group from E.41. The same weight argument proves that all differences between lifts are unramified outside3 and that no additional lower-section stabilizer acts.

# E.40: all higher central fibers and the next exact fiber group

Every nonempty lifting fiber from the full intrinsic quotient N/gamma_(r+1)N to N/gamma_rN, for r>=3, is evaluated by a finite matrix over Z3. Every central cocycle is therefore unramified away3, even when the lower section is ramified. The first nonzero graded coordinate similarly excludes every geometric stabilizer of a lower section. For a full abelian section alpha and every prime of K=Q(zeta3) away3, its tame inertia value lies in the toric rank-eight lattice. The proof preserves derived inverse limits: it first takes finite-support localization at finite coefficient level, then takes the derived inverse limit of the actual two-term local complexes. Consequently no phantom obstruction is lost. Restriction from Q to K is injective in the three-primary obstruction group, so the criterion evaluates sections over Q itself. The Kummer input is evaluated by division of explicit framed points over finite fields; it does not assume an unexplained arithmetic action obtained from the toric minimum.

The complete fiber is a torsor under R_{p!=3}(Z3) direct sum Z3^24 direct sum (Z/3)^9 direct sum (Z/9)^5. The restricted prime coordinates are actual central Kummer twists kappa(p)w. For the unramified component, the two conjugate Frobenii at7 freely generate the rank2 pro3 Galois group, and complex conjugation swaps them. # Finite intrinsic section classification E.35

Every geometrically abelian pro3 section class of the plus cubic which is unramified outside3 lifts to the full geometric pro3 arithmetic fundamental group. The explicit cubic Puiseux map t=s^3, u=-s, v=-(1-s^3)^(1/3) identifies its actual based action with the restriction of the base free pro3 action. Hain-Matsumoto section8.5, opened directly, states the genuine based unramifiedness needed here.

The maximal pro3 quotient is consequently free of rank2; the proof includes the finite-embedding-problem argument for this criterion. The relative quotient by C2 has 3-cohomological dimension1. Successive finite3-kernel embedding problems therefore lift every abelian section, and compactness of the finite section sets gives a continuous lift in the full geometric pro3 group.

For the exact abelian count, complex conjugation has plus4/minus6 on the rank10 Tate module, and rational 3-primary generalized-Jacobian torsion is C3 cubed by E.24. Finite coefficient H2 vanishes, and Milne Euler gives H1 order3^(6n+3) modulo3^n. This proves the claimed Z3 module structure, not only its rational rank.

### E_PROCONSTANT

# Arithmetic proconstant relative completion

E.30 is in `sections/E_proconstant.tex`; its statement ledger is `e_proconstant_results.json`.

The finite-stage kernel of the arithmetic orbifold group over S3×Gal(Q(μ_m)/Q) is the tangential semidirect product Fhat2⋊G_{Q(μ_m)}. Every unipotent representation of this kernel kills Fhat2. Continuously over Q_l, a suitable arithmetic element acts by χ_l≠1 on geometric abelianization but by a unipotent adjoint operator on the target, forcing the geometric image to vanish. Abstractly over Q, Nikolov–Segal2007 Theorem1.4 identifies Fhat2's abstract abelianization with Zhat²; choose χ−1 equal to a nonzero integer times a profinite unit. Its induced adjoint-minus-identity is both surjective and nilpotent, with the same conclusion.

Hence the exact relative completion is S3 times the arithmetic relative completion of G_Q. The section specifies its finite stages, kernel maps, completed inverse limit and universal property. Every finite-dimensional algebraic representation of a proconstant base factors through a finite quotient, so this inverse system is precisely the completion over S3×Zhat*. It kills all the Fermat geometric homology.

The statement also constructs the retaining alternative: the negatively weighted completion over algebraic G_m is F^un⋊A_l, with its geometric kernel injected. The proof gives its universal property and proves that no algebraic map from the proconstant cyclotomic base implements the infinite ℓ-adic character.

Primary-source verification: Nikolov–Segal, *On finitely generated profinite groups I*, Annals165(2007), Theorem1.4, was opened in the original publisher PDF, https://annals.math.princeton.edu/wp-content/uploads/annals-v165-n1-p05.pdf . Its citation key `dNikolovSegal2007` is in `d_refs.bib`. The weighted-completion reference is the already opened `E_HainMatsumoto`, sections2–4. All remaining conclusions are proved in E.30 from these definitions and the actual cyclotomic action.

The full structure of each ordinary Galois unipotent kernel is not claimed computed. Its exact completed inverse-system description is part of the proved result; replacing it by a free group of an asserted rank would require additional verified arithmetic cohomology input.

The source cochains, the q-normalized tame relation, the chosen Frobenius roots, all changes of roots/coboundaries, and transition maps are included. A convergent Q_ell family is bounded, so this comparison retains the exact bounded-denominator condition.

For rational numbers a,b the test has support in the union of the numerator/denominator prime supports, even when valuations cancel in ab. The odd-integral test is the literal tame-symbol prime-primary test; the rational test always vanishes for this finite-support input. ### E_QUARTIC_ODD

# E.103: complete odd-primary quartic abelian sections

## Result and exact source

The statement treats the plus Fermat quartic `x^4+y^4+z^4=0`, with the twelve coordinate cusps removed. For every odd prime, and simultaneously for the prime-to-two product of coefficient rings, it constructs an actual section of the arithmetic abelian extension. The construction is a four-coset formula applied to the actual tangential section over `F=Q(zeta_8)`. It is defined in the original extension, is independent of a chosen lift, and is continuous on each of the four open cosets. The displayed torus-module section then moves this origin to toric value zero.

Relative to that origin, the full geometric-conjugacy section space is

`(completed F^*)^2 x ker(Norm F/Q) x R^3`.

The actual coordinate-unit map is `(a,b,k,e) -> (Norm(a),Norm(b))`. It is onto, and the complete fibre over any prescribed toric pair is `ker(Norm)^3 x R^3`. Both directions are given by the literal norm section and division by its values. For the Fermat covering coordinate `t=-(x/z)^4`, the corresponding Kummer pair is four times this map.

## Coefficient comparisons and evaluated factors

The three homogeneous degree-four maps to `E_+: y^2=x^3+x` satisfy `Phi Psi=Psi Phi=[4]`; their differentials are an explicit basis. The generalized Jacobian extension is killed by four through the unique map `j` with `p j=[4]`. These identities supply the actual odd Tate splitting and its inverse, including all factors of four and sixteen. The eleven-dimensional torus splitting and inverse are retained as exact rational matrices with denominators powers of two, and all four Galois permutations are checked.

The elliptic factors are evaluated rather than left as named cohomology groups. The explicit two-isogeny to `y^2=x^3-4x` identifies the L-functions. The original Martin--Ono eta-product formula gives a positive Mellin integral at one. An elementary two-isogeny descent proves algebraic rank zero and rational torsion `C2`. Ciperiani--Stix Theorem 18(2) then gives the maximal divisible Weil--Chatelet group `Q/Z`. A fixed isomorphism to that subgroup, followed by the compatible Kummer inverses, gives each odd Tate cohomology factor exactly `R`, with its actual inverse map and topology.

The Shapiro map is written at cochain level, with the inverse, coboundary sign and literal norm trace. The field has class number one and units `mu_8 x <1+sqrt(2)>`. Thus the whole section space is `R^6 x restricted_sequences(I,R)` and each toric fibre is `R^6 x restricted_sequences(I',R)`. The primewise multiplicities are respectively `2,11,5` and `0,9,3`, for the prime two, primes one modulo eight, and the remaining odd primes. The topology is uniform congruence convergence of the prime-indexed sequences; arbitrary product topology is not substituted.

All rational abelian coefficient pushouts, including the two-adic one, are also evaluated. Compactness of the Galois source bounds every continuous rational cocycle, proving the exact scalar-extension map on cohomology. Their spaces are `Q_l^6 x c_0(I,Q_l)` and the corresponding fibres use `I'`. The real obstruction of E.18 is transported explicitly under rationalization. Integral section targets retaining the two-primary abelian quotient remain empty. No odd nonabelian lift is inferred from these coefficient computations.

### E_RIEMANN

# E.39: the exact arithmetic free-inertia comparison

The arithmetic Riemann-existence route was checked against Wingberg's original author PDF, Proposition 2.1 and Theorem 2.4. For K=Q(zeta3), S the prime above3, the theorem gives the continuous free pro3 product of the actual inertia groups over primes of K_S(3) outsideS as the exact kernel L of G_K(3) -> G_K,S(3). Every factor is Z3. The topology is retained: maps out of this product are continuous bundle maps, with finite support at each finite level.

The free rank2 quotient admits a split chosen by the two conjugate actual Frobenius lifts above7. The theorem then records the exact reconstruction of a section cocycle from a homomorphism lambda:L->W and a cocycle on the free quotient, including the required operator compatibility. It also records the actual descent to Q and the residual geometric conjugation action.

The key new finding is that this splitting does not erase local Frobenius correction words. Define h_q=F_q s(sigma_q)^-1. The local section equation contains lambda(h_q), multiplied with the unramified cocycle value. For every possible splitting, the claim that all h_q lie in their local inertia groups contradicts the cyclic cubic Kummer character of2 and abelian Chebotarev: its restriction along the splitting is unramified outside3 but cannot have the same Frobenius values as the character ramified at2.

For the chosen split, the difference is visible at a specific prime. At the two primes over7, the characters of2,zeta,lambda have values (2,2,0) and (1,2,2). Hence kappa2 composed with the splitting equals kappa(zeta*lambda) on the rank2 quotient. At the prime (-2+3*zeta) above19, their Frobenius values are1 and2. The correction h19 has kappa2 value2, while kappa2 is zero on local inertia at19. The supplied program verifies these finite residue computations exactly.

This is a proved comparison and a proved failure of a proposed simplification. It leaves a concrete further step: evaluate the correction words and their conjugation action in the images needed for each intrinsic metabelian or marked-depth section. The source theorem supplies the exact free kernel, and the section formula displays where those additional words occur. Neither the kernel theorem nor the two unramified action matrices alone evaluate them.

# E.79: uniform S-unit field norms and exact capitulation

## Proved result

For every d≥1, the actual cyclic extensions B_(d+1)/B_d and B_(d+2)/B_d have degrees n=3 and9 and are totally ramified at all six primes above3 and17. The complete S-unit field-norm index is n^5. The proof transports the actual six Hilbert symbols from the certified first level through N(beta_d)=beta_1 and the correctly oriented local Artin norm/inclusion square. It retains the common μ9 and the full local degrees, including the non-Galois comparison B_d/B_1.

The ideal norm identifies the upper S-class coinvariants with the base S-class group. This is proved through the actual S-split Hilbert class field and its idelic quotient. On Frobenius classes, the resulting map is residue-degree multiplication, hence precisely ideal norm. The first two coinvariant groups are C3.

## Explicit first-level coordinates

The certified ordinary-unit basis and six literal S-unit generators give a complete 15-coordinate system. The actual six-valuation lattice has determinant3. The complete norm-character kernels have indices243 and59049. After ordinary-unit correction, the two S-generator exponent quotients are C3² and C9×C3; in actual six-valuation coordinates they are C3³ and C9×C3². The theorem prints the exact congruences, while the certificate retains all ten full integer Smith transformations and their inverses. Fourteen literal ordinary-unit corrections are checked.

The prime above73 with beta1 residue22 has the actual class coordinate (2,1). The compatible roots22,63,22,63,... define degree-one prime chains at all levels. Their three-primary upper classes generate the first cubic and ninth-degree S-class modules. Extension of the base class is the norm polynomial 1+sigma+...+sigma^(n−1) applied to that generator. This gives an exact capitulation test in the actual annihilator ideal; that ideal has not been evaluated.

## Field norms and actual S-unit norms

For u=Nx that is an S-unit, the divisor of x outsideS is (sigma−1)D. The class of D modulo extended base classes gives the exact obstruction. Hilbert90 proves independence of the chosen x, and invariant divisors are exactly extended base divisors because ramification is confined toS. The kernel consists precisely of actual S-unit norms. A second explicit Hilbert90 map identifies the capitulation kernel with H1 of the upper S-unit group. The S-unit Herbrand quotient n^5 then proves that the defect has exactly the order of capitulation.

At the first level this defect has order at most3, so cubing every S-unit field norm gives an actual S-unit norm. The literal c1 corrections are included. The uncorrected c1 has a nontrivial tame cubic residue, while multiplication by the specified ordinary units corrects all six local characters. No field-norm preimage is silently treated as an S-unit preimage.

All S-unit groups here are finitely generated. Their finite-index kernels and norm images therefore retain the stated exact sequences upon completion for multiplication by3^m. The all-level coherent norm system remains a separate, explicit inverse-system condition through E.46/E.72.
