### E_SECOND_NORM

# E.62 — A second positional norm obstruction and the complete local tower

Status: Proved; independent full-proof and fresh copied-source execution audit PASS by a_structure (identical JSON). Root also read the complete proof and reran the program: PASS, no correction. The independent audit is recorded in ledger/a_independent_audits.md.

## Global finite positional obstruction

Fix the actual first cocycle A=3 kappa_3(17) in the genuine quotient H from E.38/E.46. At the first positional level the algebra is Q times K, K=Q(zeta3). The class(1,zeta3) has total norm one and therefore satisfies the actual J-cohomology condition.

E.57 supplies a literal lift at the next level: (1,w), with w=(g1/pi)^2 in L1, beta1^3=17zeta3, and N_(L1/K)w=zeta3. At the following level the complex component is L2=K(beta2), beta2^9=17zeta3. Its completion at17 is totally ramified of degree nine overK17 and has residue fieldF289. The norm of a unit reduces to a ninth power. That image has order32, so it excludes the residue ofzeta3, of order three. This finite residue quotient also detects the failure in the completions for multiplication by3^m. Thus no choice of the first lift extends further.

The actual maps are H toH3 toH2 toH1, with Hn the quotient by0 times(T^(3^n)-1)Lambda. The obstruction is to the indicated positional class. Its toric pair is(kappa17,0), which also occurs for the literal full global section g->(A(g),0,g). The comparison therefore explicitly exhibits two positional classes with the same toric pair and different lifting behavior.

## Complete local section fibers at seventeen

The pure local tower Fd=Q17(17^(1/3^d)) is totally ramified with residue fieldF17. The complex tower Ld=Q17(zeta3,(17zeta3)^(1/3^d)) is totally ramified overK17, with residue fieldF289. Their completed multiplicative groups are respectively Z3 and Z3 plusC9. The compatible uniformizers have exact norm equal to the preceding uniformizer; the common ninth roots have norm equal to their cubes.

Consequently each local finite positional fiber is the explicit group

    {(e,f) inZ3^2:e+2f belongs to3Z3} plus C9,

with transition(e,f,t)->(e,f,3t). The full local fiber is the displayed lattice, since the inverse limit of C9 under cubing is zero. It maps to the first positional coordinates as(17^e,(17zeta3)^f).

The toric quotient divides both raw coordinates by three. Its value is

    (kappa17, ((e+2f)/3)kappa17).

The inverse parametrization(e,f)=(3b-2f,f) proves that every second toric coordinate occurs and each toric fiber is exactlyZ3. The local classification includes the full geometric-conjugacy quotient: there is no remaining first-coordinate stabilizer because the cyclotomic Tate module has no fixed vectors.

# E.56: complete mixed first fibers at a split prime

Status: Proved; independent full proof and fresh-execution audit PASS. The proof and certificates are installed in the deliverable after the checkpoint freeze was released.

## Exact scope

Let ell be a rational prime with v_3(ell-1)=1 and with three a noncube modulo ell. For every a != 0 and b in Z_3, E.56 evaluates every global section class of the actual inherited quotient H with first cocycle class 3a kappa_3(ell)+3b kappa_3(3).

Writing s=v_3(3a), the complete coordinates are Z_3^2 times the restricted sequence module on the explicitly eligible primes, times Z_3^(2s), times Z_3[[Z]]. The two unrestricted scalar coordinates and the restricted prime coordinates give exactly the scaled second toric Kummer coordinate. Each toric fiber is the explicitly parametrized compact group Z_3^(2s) times Z_3[[Z]]. The entire fixed-first fiber is torsion-free.

An outside prime p is eligible exactly when the three-primary component of ell^(3a) 3^(3b) modulo p is trivial. The displayed finite congruence uses a and b only modulo 3^max(v_3(p-1)-1,0), so the condition is defined for every three-adic a and b.

## Arithmetic comparison maps

The proof chooses actual Frobenius and tame inertia generators at the two primes of K=Q(zeta_3) over ell. An explicit conjugation makes the two Frobenius Kummer values of three exactly +1 and -1; both Frobenii fix the compatible roots of ell. Their Frattini determinant is a three-adic unit, so their images form a free basis of G_(K,{3})^(3).

Wingberg's opened Theorem 2.4 supplies the continuous inertia kernel. The induced inertia bundles give inverse maps identifying G_(K,{ell,3})^(3) with the free pro-three product of the two actual local tame groups. The same actual local inclusions give a complex-conjugation-equivariant section into the full relative arithmetic group. The proof states both bundle relations and maps rather than inferring a presentation from generator counts.

For the fixed first cocycle, the additive coefficient module is J=(3,T-1)Z_3[[T-1]]. Complex conjugation exchanges the two local factors. After normalizing its cocycle value to zero, the entire finite-ramification calculation reduces to one explicit tame relation and the exact residual odd-series conjugations.

The finite quotient is the actual cyclic group ring Z_3[C_(3^s)]. Multiplication by T^(3b/(ell-1)) removes the affine shift. Multiplication by ell has exactly 2s+1 finite orbits. Centered representatives have first moments +A/3 and -A/3, which compute the second augmentation and impose exactly one index-three condition. Equation E56.8 solves that condition with an explicit integral basis. The residual odd series is removed by an allowed geometric conjugation; the remaining even series is uniquely a power series in T+T^(-1)-2. Its constant is fixed by the prescribed second toric coordinate.

Outside ell and three the actual free inertia kernel acts trivially on J. Its equivariant additive maps are precisely the restricted families of the integral fixed Dirac measures. Each residue cocycle has a displayed toric correction kappa_3(p)-kappa_3(p)(sigma) kappa_3(3). Adding the specified unramified normal form produces a cocycle with exactly kappa_3(p) as its second toric image. This supplies the full global reconstruction and its inverse.

The comparison with E.46 is through its actual finite Kummer-Shapiro maps, followed by inertia extraction, finite orbit extraction, principal-ideal division and even projection. The reverse construction is the stated arithmetic cocycle and its corresponding norm system. The maps are inverse at every finite level and continuous in the established topologies. The restricted sequence module retains its noncompact inverse-limit topology.

The intrinsic Fermat group, its metabelian quotient and its marked-depth quotient still map to H through the exact kernels in E47.8. E.56 computes the target fibers; source lifting conditions remain attached to those kernels.

E.26 proves that the abelian splitting does not lift integrally or profinitely even to the class-two quotient. With the original modular generators and x=(sigma rho)^2, y=(rho sigma)^2, the exact free-group identities are (x rho)^3=[x,y] and ((x rho)sigma)^2=1. The preimage of the unique abelian complement is the infinite dihedral extension of S3 by Z(sign), of class 1 modulo3.

The explicit quotient P/(gamma_3(F) F^3) has 162 elements. The program constructs its regular permutation group from all 162 Hall normal forms, checks closure, all 4,251,528 associativity triples, and all 729 generator pairs for sections. It finds zero sections. The abelian quotient has 54 elements and nine sections; all 81 lift pairs give an order-nine lift of the order-three generator. The preimage of the chosen complement is D18. Every normalized one-cochain modulo3 was checked against the explicit carry cocycle, with no solution.

Exact quotient maps propagate the obstruction to the metabelian and third-derived extensions. The relative pro-p class-two extension has zero section classes for p=3, two for p=2, and one for p>=5. The rational class-two extension has a section obtained by multiplying x rho by c^(-1/3), with a single conjugacy class. The marked-depth quotient of E.2 has an explicit surjection to the class-two Lie quotient; the permutation swap does not preserve its defining ideal, as a displayed degree-four monomial proves.

norm square proves exact equality of the character on every
included unit at consecutive stages. Global reciprocity bounds
the image by the five-dimensional hyperplane, so the image is
exactly that hyperplane at all stages fromB3.

vertical action on the entire B class group is trivial, the
ambiguous formula fixes every later class number at243.

intersection/Frobenius map, hence are isomorphisms. The six named

## Coherent tuple and phase

For the E88 restricted divisor kernel K, the remaining phase

For first class kappa17 and pure positional component1, the
second toric class may have arbitrary coefficients B17,B3

### E_TAME_CLASSES

# E.45: complete local H section classes

## Completed classification

The actual arithmetic module quotient H=3Z3 semidirect(3,U)Z3[[U]] now has a complete local section classification over each K-prime away from3. The full geometric conjugation formula preserves the actual inertia coordinates a,c, and normalizes b,d uniquely in the multiples of3 between0 and3^(v3(q-1)+1)-3. The residual conjugation group is exactly UZ3[[U]].

For nonzero first inertia, E.42's finite two-row Smith system and its explicit representatives give all classes. For zero first inertia, the remaining operator D_b=qT^b psi_q-1 is completely evaluated on the augmentation ideal.

When r=b/(q-1) is integral, the coordinate group is the full product of Z3 indexed by j>=0 and units u modulo3^v3(q-1). The actual representative is w=d+T^(-r) sum z_j,u(T^(3^j u)-1), with v=cT^(-r). The series converges in the actual profinite completed-group-ring topology, equivalently (3,U)-adically. It is not claimed to converge U-adically with discrete coefficients.

When r is nonintegral, c must be0. Put t=v3(b). The coordinate group is the rank3^t lattice of vectors whose sum is divisible byq-1, with basis(q-1)e0 and e_i-e0. It has actual representatives w=d+z0 q(1-T^b)+sum z_i(T^i-1), v=0.

## Exact maps

The proof identifies the completed group ring with integral measures, not with a changed coefficient object. Multiplication by T^r conjugates D_b to D_0 in the integral-r case. Restriction to the clopen free q-orbits gives a topological product decomposition of the augmentation ideal; the inverse is the centered-measure series. Each orbit quotient is evaluated by the actual weighted integral integral q^(-n) dmu, whose kernel is multiplication byqS-1.

For nonintegral r, the affine action qx+b has exactly3^t free compact orbits. Weighted integrals give the full group-ring cokernel. Their sum moduloq-1 is augmentation, and a direct preimage construction proves the precise augmentation-kernel lattice, including injectivity. All quotient and representative maps are continuous between compact Hausdorff modules.

## Executed finite verification

`code/e_tame_classes.py`, `.out`, `.json` are complete. The program verifies48 finite coinvariant systems and36 actual transition maps for q=4,7,19,37,109 and several translations b. It computes the augmentation-ideal matrix in the basis delta_j-delta_0, verifies its exact Smith valuations against the orbit formula, and checks that both representative families generate the finite quotient.

For transitions, it checks group-ring reduction intertwines D_b and verifies the weighted-integral coefficients on every Dirac mass. Three full modular Smith transformation certificates are retained. The infinite centered-series convergence is also checked directly at every tested group-ring level, where all terms with j>=k vanish.

The all-degree result follows from the written integral measure and orbit proof. No finite experiment is used to infer an unproved infinite identity.

## Scope

Their additional lifting conditions remain the actual E.37/E.44 tests. The proof does not assert that every H section lifts intrinsically.

For every nonzero three-adic integer r, write r=3^(m−1)e with m≥1 and e a unit. The theorem evaluates every global section class of the actual arithmetic quotient H whose first class is 3r κ₃(2). The section group has coordinates

Here R(P_m) consists of sequences tending to zero modulo every power of three, with its multiplication-by-powers-of-three inverse-limit topology. It is not asserted compact. The remaining displayed factors are compact. The scaled toric map keeps precisely b₂ κ₃(2)+b₃ κ₃(3)+Σ b_p κ₃(p), together with the fixed first coordinate r κ₃(2). Every toric fiber has the explicit compact coordinates Z₃^(h+m) × ∏_(n≥m) Z₃^(3^n). The finite coordinate concatenation producing h+m is displayed in the theorem.

## Arithmetic proof

The cofinal actual etale algebras factor as the real field F_d=Q(2^(1/3^d)) and the m fields

L_(d,j)=Q(2^(1/3^d) ζ_(3^(d+j))), 1≤j≤m.

The transition is cubing of the indicated generators. Every complex tower begins at Q(ζ_(3^j)). Two is inert in that cyclotomic base because its order modulo3^j is φ(3^j). Every vertical cubic layer is totally ramified at its unique two-prime. The primitive root ζ_(3^j) has noncube residue in the fixed residue field, so it is not a norm of an element in any vertical layer.

The opened Lemmermeyer Theorem1 gives both the ambiguous and strongly ambiguous class-number formulas. Only two and three may ramify. The non-norm unit, together with a class number prime to three at the previous layer, forces ramification at three and forces each norm index to be exactly three. An ambiguous three-class would exist whenever the new three-class group were nonzero, so the latter vanishes. This simultaneously proves unique total ramification at three, vanishing of all three-class groups, and the exact actual unit norm image. The real factor follows by the degree-two extension/norm maps and the explicit projector (1+c)/2.

The image of the unit norm is the residue-cube subgroup. On the kernel of the three-primary residue map the norm is onto: a unit preimage has residue of order dividing three and can be multiplied by ζ₃ to remove that residue without changing its norm. The residue group in the jth tower is µ_(3^j), with cubing transitions. Its norm inverse limit is zero, and the images stabilize after j steps to the residue-one unit subgroup. There is no omitted inverse-limit term.

These residue-one groups are torsion-free. Their ranks and the real unit ranks add to

R_d=(3^(m+d)−1)/2−m,

and the successive norm kernels have rank3^(m+d). Normalized fundamental-unit bases and their actual integer norm matrices give the full unit parameter product.

## Explicit coordinate maps

Each norm matrix is onto over Z₃. The theorem chooses the first maximal minor with determinant prime to three, forms its rational right inverse, and constructs a kernel basis by subtracting the lifted pivot part from each nonpivot coordinate. Every resulting denominator is prime to three. These finite matrices recursively construct the norm-coherent units and recover their unique coordinates.

At two there are m+1 surviving prime chains, with total norm weights 1,φ(3),…,φ(3^m). At three there are m+1 chains, all of weight one. The prime-two norm systems are the literal generators α_d and β_(d,j). For the real factor and the first complex factor, the prime-three systems are literally1+α_d and1+β_(d,1), with exact cubic norms and absolute norm3.

Outside these two bad primes, the affine Frobenius on positions is x↦px+A_F. Its only possible invariant integral measure is a Dirac mass at its unique integral fixed point. Such a fixed point exists exactly at P_m. Finite positional orbit sums are the actual prime divisors and their pushforward is multiplication by relative residue degree, so this computes the full ideal norm limit.

Every prescribed compatible divisor system lifts. At a complex factor, if r_d is the root-of-unity component of the norm defect, the explicit finite product

ρ_d=∏_(a=0..j−1) r_(d+a)^(3^a)

satisfies ρ_d=r_d ρ_(d+1)^3. It removes the residue obstruction. The remaining residue-one defects are removed by the onto unit norm maps. This gives genuine norm systems for every allowed prime chain. Arbitrary restricted sequences of their powers converge at every finite coefficient exponent.

The total norm cube condition gives precisely the stated toric image and the two blocks of m relative valuations. For e≠1 the arithmetic automorphism (a,v(T))↦(ea,v(T^e)) transports the first class and preserves augmentation, hence preserves the second toric coordinate. This evaluates the full nonzero κ₃(2) line.

An S-unit x would make y an ordinary unit, which is impossible. For arbitrary field x the outside-S divisor is retained; its horizontal norm maps the B2 S-class defect to (0,1). The established class map z↦(0,z) fixes the sign +1 of the original defect.

toric coordinate is exactly b3 kappa_3(3) plus the restricted sum of the
prime Kummer classes. The allowed primes are precisely p other than3
with v3(ord_p(3)) at most k+1. E.47 already evaluates the zero fiber,
so together these results cover every first class unramified outside3.

unit part of r. It also transports the finite root torsors by i to e i;
the induced isomorphisms of finite etale algebras identify the exact

the inversion-fixed part of Z3[C_M] and the remaining series T^M Wf(W²).
Its proof divides only on specified closed principal ideals and retains
all (M-1)/2 finite augmentation-zero parameters.

The localization comparison uses the finite arithmetic modules themselves:
their action over Q(zeta3) factors through the free unramified pro-three
group, and their filtration by trivial finite three-modules gives the
required higher-cohomology vanishing. Finite H1 then supplies the exact
inverse-limit sequence. The local invariant measures are either zero or

sequence condition follows at level zero and is sufficient at every level.

The residue lifts are also constructed in fields. In the cofinal range
n=d+k+1, the root algebra factors into Q(3^(1/3^d)) and
Q(3^(1/3^d) zeta_(3^(d+j))) for j=1,...,k+1. The actual arithmetic element
rho with cyclotomic value1 and Kummer value minus2 proves the precise
degrees of these factors. Every vertical layer containing zeta3 is cyclic

ambiguous class formulas prove that its class number has trivial
three-primary part and its unit norm is onto. The pure radical factor is
handled by its actual quadratic extension and the integral projector.
Thus each degree-one prime chain has a norm-compatible principal divisor
lift, corrected by actual unit norms, with zero valuations elsewhere.

### E_UNRAMIFIED_LOCAL

# E.68 — complete unramified local sections and exact comparison fibers

Every unramified local section is represented by an arbitrary Frobenius coordinate. Finite-field weights make every twisted stabilizer trivial. Hall collection and finite Smith representatives therefore give complete compatible coordinates, one finite cokernel of F_r−1 in each degree, for the full, metabelian and single-cusp marked groups.

The full-to-quotient fiber uses the actual closed kernel R with filtration R intersect gamma_r(Pi). The integral graded quotient is free, so the kernel is saturated. The exact fiber over a representative z_Q is H1(Z3,R,Ad(z0)F), with the literal map r to r*z0*F. It has a product of the kernel cokernels as compatible coordinates. No full ramified section lies over an unramified target, because the quotients preserve the abelianization and every full ramified section has nonzero cusp inertia.

The comparison map is fully specified. Its degree-r coordinate is the map on finite cokernels plus the correction obtained by lifting the unique lower conjugator and evaluating the genuine nonlinear Frobenius on that lift. The next aligning conjugator is the unique integral inverse of the target F_r−1 on its actual image. A recursive finite-set section of each cokernel surjection then parametrizes every fiber. These formulas retain the nonlinear corrections and every representative choice.

## Exact finite evaluation at residue cardinality four

The actual E37 Frobenius lattice is integrally conjugate to two rank-two blocks and six scalar Tate lines, by a matrix fixing the marked B0 generator. Through degree four this gives invariant multidegree blocks of size at most24. All first-four-degree factors are evaluated in the theorem. Their ranks are990 and9, and determinant valuations1138 and9. The full degree-four determinant valuation2851 equals the respective quotient valuations1713 or2842 plus the kernel value.

The independent nonlinear test uses the actual E54 quotient. Five hundred exact rational3-adic normalizations recover both representative and unique conjugator. With the specified shifted target Hall lifts the genuine triangular correction is1+a−b modulo3. All2187 source representatives map into27 target classes with81 representatives in each fiber. Every assertion passed in a fresh execution.

Its residual conjugation action is w x tau(w)^(-1), w in W^c.

Each successive parameter is the explicit cokernel of (T_r-1)(1+J_r), computed from the complete rank-ten V action. No further stabilizer quotient occurs. Continuous Hall-zero lifts and Smith representatives produce a compatible normal form in every degree. Compactness and the vanishing of every twisted stabilizer prove the inverse-limit bijection and a noncanonical homeomorphism of pointed profinite spaces with the product of these cokernels. The product does not assert a group law on the original section set.

## Integral comparisons proved

The metabelian Fox comparison is proved in the pro-3 category. The completed group algebra of a free pro-3 group is identified with the complete noncommutative power-series ring, with its actual (3,augmentation)-adic topology. The last-letter resolution, completed induced-module comparison and continuous Fox map identify N'/N'' with the closed syzygy kernel in Lambda^10. For the single-cusp marked quotient, Magnus expansions show that gamma3(I_Q) has at least three occurrences of the marked variable. Integral Lazard elimination supplies the reverse inclusion of initial ideals, by actual lifted commutators. The retained Hall words form a torsion-free integral basis in every degree.

## Proof points

The first local Kummer calculation fixes an actual relative Frattini basis before any source change. The characters of u2 and u3 form the relative dual basis; their values on an element and its conjugate have the exact matrix written in the proof.

The cover is multiplication by three from E minus E[3] to E minus its
origin, with the tangent sent from v to 3v. The full induced pro-three
topology identifies its group with the homology-modulo-three kernel of
the free rank-two group. Filling the punctures gives exactly three times
the elliptic Tate-module map. This proves the arithmetic equivariance and
the primitive rank-eight cusp lattice used in the calculation.

The passage from Lie coordinates to the actual group kernel was checked
separately. In the intrinsic class-three group, the image J of the ambient
commutator group is H times gamma2(C), where H is generated by the eight
cusp basis elements. Its derived subgroup is B times L3. The 28 actual
generators of B have independent degree-two Hall coordinates, hence B has
trivial intersection with gamma3(C); L3 is central and has rank304. Thus
the actual kernel is the abelian group Z3^332. This proof uses integral
group collection and does not divide by three.

The retained quotient coordinates have ranks10,17,26. Their integral
saturation follows from the exact right inverse, and the normality follows

The two subsequently appended transfers were also checked. The equality

therefore has the stated abelian kernel, with actual Hall ranks16 and26.

Their finite-image discussion on pp.573--574 explicitly retains an intersection over finer finite quotients; it gives no stopping bound for the refinement procedure. We opened the original author-hosted scan and the proof in Section3. These descriptions recover the conjugator equations used here. They do not supply the compatible conjugators and generation conditions in the remaining finer quotients of D.22.

Drinfeld, *Leningrad Mathematical Journal*2 (1991), Section4, constructs the arrows from the full profinite group to its pro-l and rational pro-unipotent versions. Propositions5.5--5.9 concern the rational pro-unipotent associator space and its graded comparison; Proposition5.8 proves successive lifting there. The final remark in Section6 compares the degree-seven graded Lie spaces. We checked these statements in the original English translation. The required comparison in D.13 goes in the reverse direction from the constructed profinite-to-pro-l arrow, and a peripheral full profinite lift is still required. No reverse lifting result is inferred from those passages.

The additional original-source search for Ihara1986 and Coleman1989 reached the Annals publisher record, JSTOR issue metadata, the MSJ volume17 catalogue and the NDL record. No readable original proof was obtained on those routes. Their original-proof verification status remains unverified; later theorem attributions are not substituted for opening the originals.

The public OpenAlex metadata supplied a separate content URL for Coleman1989, `https://content.openalex.org/works/W2888189952.pdf`. A normal unauthenticated request returned HTTP401. The paper was not obtained through that endpoint, and no access-control bypass was attempted.

pairs in that completion. Chapter 12 constructs the profinite arithmetic

Horel's original *Profinite completion of operads and the
Grothendieck–Teichmüller group*, Theorem 7.2, Corollary 7.3 and

as units in their respective equation monoids and constructs natural

full profinite Main Theorem and the Belyi subgroup definition; those give
the exact full-source interpretation already used in the paper.

# Independent proof review of D.34 and D.35

The root agent read both complete proof files and checked the directions of
their comparison maps. The faithful mod-two deck representation and the
trivial free-group center give the embedding of the relative group into
Aut(K2); its image is precisely the inverse image of the specified outer
deck subgroup. Conjugation by an outer-centralizing automorphism is the
explicit inverse to restriction. The primitive cusp and its mod-two
stabilizer give the whole centralizer and both components of the normalized
boundary exponent.

The filling matrix has the two displayed primitive cusp vectors as its
kernel. The central order-three idempotent supplies the integral splitting;
the relative surface class supplies the determinant equation. The explicit
curve isomorphism has its stated field of definition, and applying Galois
to that isomorphism gives the retained cubic twist on Tate modules. The
identification of the CM coefficient ring sends zeta3 squared to the matrix
of t squared, as recorded by the actual deck map.

For D.35 the elementary boundary automorphisms, their inverse words, the
handle operations and four cusp shears give the entire asserted homology
image. The integral-homology kernel is pro-two. Coprime complement conjugacy
inside its actual preimage supplies the odd-deck correction without changing
the homology or boundary multiplier. The normalized representative map and
the primitive-cusp centralizer give exactly the procyclic inner ambiguity
in the reduction to the prescribed finite D.33 action.

The arithmetic strengthening uses actual Frobenius elements. Rational
two-torsion puts the E3 image inside 1+2O; the point counts and conjugate
CM roots at 7 and 13 generate that group's full quotient modulo 8. Every
element of 1+8O is a square in 1+2O, so this finite computation surjects
onto its Frattini quotient and proves the entire principal-unit image.
The cubic radical field and the pro-two division field are disjoint over
Q(zeta3), which proves the joint image and then the full twisted compact
torus image. The deck maps are all defined over that field, so this actual
Galois action centralizes the full outer deck group. Inner normalization
retains its compact Tate action.

These arguments do not assign the second CM coordinate or the complete
retained finite D.33 action. The exact unresolved fiber is retained in
D.35.5a and in the original open tasks. The separate copied-source
certificate audit is recorded in the independent agent ledger.

## Files and reproduction

The ZIP includes the LuaLaTeX master source and section sources, PDF, required CSV ledgers, combined bibliography, this report, exact mathematical programs, saved standard output, and machine-readable certificates. Published papers and raw third-party HTML are reading aids and are not redistributed. The small-group input retains source URLs and hashes.

See code/README.md for commands, dependencies and the distinction between mathematical computations, ledger assembly and document compilation. code/assemble.py verifies statement/status correspondence, complete task coverage and citation-key coverage. The PDF build rejects undefined citations/references, missing characters and overfull boxes. Visual inspection is recorded separately after rendering.
